2022 年是極不平凡的一年，國內外經濟環境受到   During this exceptional year of 2022, various unstable economic
多方面不穩定因素衝擊，給整個市場以及氟硅化      factors had struck both the domestic and global market, causing
工行業帶來許多不確定因素，這同時也給本集團      significant uncertainties to the fluorosilicon chemical industry and the
帶來了機遇和挑戰。在機遇和挑戰來臨時，本集      overall market. Such uncertainties had also brought both
團憑藉先進的技術、優質的產品質量、完善的產      opportunities and challenges to the Group. Through our advanced
業鏈等優勢，牢牢把握市場變化帶來的機會，為      technology, high product quality and a comprehensive industrial
本集團業績帶來大幅增長，創造了本集團成立以      chain, the Group firmly seized all these opportunities and challenges
來的最佳業績，為本集團 35 周年慶交出一份滿意   arisen from market volatility and brought substantial growth to the
的答卷。                       Group’s financial performance, helping the Group achieve the best
                           financial results in the Group’s history, a satisfactory response to the
                           35th anniversary of the Group.

6.   房地產業務逐步退出，明確未來發展方向                6.   Gradual withdrawal of real estate business to define future

向海外供應商購買機器及設備結算時均收取╱支                   dollars) were received/paid when the Group earned revenue from
付外幣（主要為美元）。                             overseas customers and when settling purchases of machinery and
                                        equipment from the overseas suppliers.

為減少持有外幣的風險，本集團經考慮不久將來                   In order to reduce the risk of holding foreign currencies, the Group
的外幣付款安排後，通常會在款項收訖後隨即將                   normally converts the foreign currencies into RMB upon receipt while
外幣兌換成人民幣。                               taking into account its foreign currencies payment schedule in the
                                        near future.

董事會採納的股息政策旨在讓本公司於建議股息              The dividend policy adopted by the Board is intended for the
時，維持足夠現金儲備以應付其營運及資金需求，             Company to maintain adequate cash reserves to meet its operational
為未來增長提供資金，以及如有派息時，提高股              and capital requirements, fund its future growth and enhance
東價值，作出派息須符合適用法律、規則及規例              shareholder value if and when dividends are recommended subject to
以及本公司的章程。根據股息政策，董事會宣派              applicable laws, rules and regulations and the articles of association of
任何股息視乎以下因素，包括本集團的實際及預              the Company. According to the dividend policy, the declaration of
期財務表現、本集團的可供分派儲備、本集團的              any dividends will depend on the following factors. including actual
營運資金及資本開支需求、本集團的流動資金狀              and expected financial performance of the Group, the distributable
況、整體經濟狀況及董事會認為相關的任何其他              reserves of the Group, the working capital and capital expenditure
因素。董事會在推薦建議截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日     requirements of the Group, the liquidity position of the Group, the
止年度的末期股息時已考慮相關因素。                  overall economic situation, and any other factors that the Board
                                   considers relevant. The Board has considered the relevant factors
                                   when recommending the final dividends for the year ended 31
                                   December 2022.

本公司擬通過利用配售新股的淨收益增加 20,000 噸∕年的 PTFE                                    The Company intended to increase the production capacity of PTFE by 20,000-ton/year
產能，其中一般即 10,000 噸∕年已在 2022 年達標。然而，2022                                 using the net proceeds from the placing of new shares, half of which, being 10,000-ton/
年 PTFE 市場變化錯綜複雜，下游需求較弱，PTFE 市場存在著重                                     year has already been achieved in 2022. However, due to the complex changes in the
大不確定性，所以尚未完成的 10,000 噸∕年 PTFE 項目將由 2022                                PTFE market and weaker downstream demand, significant uncertainties exist in the PTFE
年 12 月 31 日延期至 2025 年 12 月 31 日，以便本集團在合適的時                             market, and accordingly, the outstanding 10,000-ton/year PTFE project will be postponed
機進行市場佈局，降低市場變化對本集團造成的影響，從而降                                            from 31 December 2022 to 31 December 2025, in order to enable the Group to conduct
低經營風險。                                                                 market deployment at the appropriate time to reduce the impact of market changes on
                                                                       the Group, thereby reducing operational risk.

主席與行政總裁的角色                      Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守則第二部分第 C.2.1 條規定，主席與行政總裁的      Code C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman
角色應有區分，且不應由同一人兼任。張建宏先           and chief executive officer should be separate and should not be
生目前身兼本公司主席與行政總裁。董事會認為，          performed by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the
由一人同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團更能           Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board
貫徹強勢領導，且在業務策劃上更具效率。此外，          believes that vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive
董事會認為，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹的人士           Officer in the same position provides the Group with stronger and
所組成董事會密切監控下，此架構不會影響權力           more consistent leadership and allows for more effective planning.
平衡。董事會對張先生充滿信心，並深信現時安           Further, the Board considers that this structure will not impair the
排對本集團的業務前景最為有利。                 balance of power, which has been closely monitored by the Board,
                                which comprises experienced and high caliber individuals. The Board
                                has full confidence in Mr. Zhang and believes that the current
                                arrangement is beneficial to the business prospect of the Group.

於董事會層面的性別多元化                      Gender diversity at board level
守則附錄 14J 規定，董事會應不遲於 2024 年 12 月   Appendix 14J of the Code stipulates that the board should achieve
31 日實現性別多元化。本公司為單一性別（男性）          gender diversity no later than 31 December 2024. The Company is
董事會，目前正在挑選合適的女性候選人成為董             single gender (male) board and is currently selecting suitable female
事會成員。董事會致力於通過在 2024 年 12 月 31     candidate(s) to become board member(s). The Board is endeavor to
日之前任命至少一名女性董事以實現董事會性別             achieve the board gender diversity by appointment at least one female

A.   政策聲明                         A.   Policy Statement
     本公司明白並深信建立一個多元的董事會的               The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a
     裨益良多。一個真正多元的董事會可容入並               diverse Board. A truly diverse Board will include and make good
     善用董事的不同技能、地區及行業經驗、背               use of differences in the skills, regional and industry experience,
     景、種族、性別及其他資歷。此等差異將用               background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors. These
     作決定董事會的最佳組合。董事會所有的委               differences will be taken into account in determining the
     任均以用人唯才為原則，並於考慮人選時以               optimum composition of the Board. All Board appointments will
     客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員多元化的裨益。              be based on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered
                                       against objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of
                                       diversity of the Board.

B.   可測量目標                        B.   Measurable Objectives
     1. 提名委員會將就實施董事會成員多元               1.  The Nomination Committee will discuss and agree
        化每年討論及議定可測量目標，並向                   annually measurable objectives for implementing diversity
        董事會作出建議以供採納。                       on the Board and recommend to the Board for adoption.

     2.   本公司旨在建立並維持董事會董事在             2.    The Company aims to build and maintain a Board with a
          技 能、經 驗、知 識、專 長、文 化、獨              diversity of Directors, in terms of skills, experiences,
          立身份、年齡及性別方面的多元化。                   knowledge, expertise, culture, independence, age and
                                             gender.

C.   監察及匯報                        C.   Monitoring and Reporting
     1. 提名委員會每年將於企業管治報告內               1.   The Nomination Committee will report annually, in the
        從多元化角度匯報董事會的組成，並                    Corporate Governance Report, on the Board’s
        監察政策的實施情況。                          composition under diversified perspectives, and monitor
                                            the implementation of the Policy.

     2.   政策概要連同就實施政策而制定的可             2.    A summary of the Policy, together with the measurable
          測量目標，以及在達成該等目標上取                   objectives set for implementing the Policy and the
          得的進展，將於企業管治報告內作出                   progress made towards achieving those objectives will be
          披露。                                disclosed in the Corporate Governance Report.

     提名委員會將於適當時候檢討政策，並向董               The Nomination Committee will review the Policy, as
     事會提出任何擬定更改建議以供審批。                 appropriate, and recommend any proposed changes to the

董事及外聘核數師各自的責任              Respective responsibilities of Directors and
                           external auditors
董事負責編製真實公平反映本集團財務狀況及本      The Directors are responsible for the preparation of the financial
集團業績及現金流量的財務報表。外聘核數師則      statements, which give a true and fair view of the state of affairs of
負責根據審核結果，對董事編製的財務報表提出      the Group and of the Group’s results and cash flows. The external
獨立意見，並僅向本公司股東報告，除此之外不      auditors are responsible to form an independent opinion, based on
可作其他用途。                    the audit, on the financial statements prepared by the Directors and
                           report the opinion solely to the members of the Company as a body
                           and for no other purpose.

風險管理及內部監控                  Risk management and internal control
董事會負責評估及釐定本集團達成策略目標時所      The Board of Directors is responsible for assessing and determining
願意接納的風險性質及程度，並確保本集團設立      the nature and extent of the risks that the Group is willing to accept
及維持合適及有效的風險管理及內部監控系統。      in reaching its strategic objectives and to ensure that the Group has
董事會已監督管理層對風險管理及內部監控系統      established and maintained appropriate and effective risk
的設計、實施及監察。董事會確認，其有責任為      management and internal control systems. The Board of Directors has
本集團建立風險管理及內部監控系統，並會持續      supervised the management’s design, implementation and monitoring
監督有關係統，並確保最少每年檢討一次公司及      of risk management and internal control systems. The Board

本聲明旨在闡釋東岳集團董事會對環境、社會及               The purpose of this statement is to explain the governance and
管治相關工作的治理和監督責任。                     supervision responsibilities of the Board of the Dongyue Group with
                                    respect to ESG related work.

東岳集團有限公司始終認為公司管治和風險管理               Dongyue Group Limited has always believed that corporate
是企業可持續發展的必要環節。集團在可持續發               governance and risk management are essential components of
展的過程中，不斷提升對環境、社會及管治三個               sustainable corporate development. In the process of sustainable
維度的關注度和投入度，將企業發展與社會、環               development, the Group continues to enhance its concern and
境高度融合，助力良好的行業發展，以自身資源               commitment to the three dimensions of ESG, integrating corporate
帶動環境改善，與社會共同發展，共創價值。                development with society and the environment, contributing to good
                                    industry development, driving environmental improvement with its
                                    own resources, developing together with society and creating value
                                    together.

     德，嚴把產品質量關，著力優化服務體驗，                         integrity and self-discipline, strictly control the quality of

     購，強化供應鏈管理，致力於打造高品質供                         solidify the foundation of responsibility. Meanwhile, the Group

     2.1   加強反腐倡廉                                2.1   Reinforcement of Anti-corruption Efforts

           東岳集團嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國公                           Dongyue Group strictly complies with relevant laws and
           司法》《中華人民共和國監察法》
                         《關於                           regulations, such as the Company Law of the People’s
           禁止商業賄賂行為的暫行規定》等法律                           Republic of China, Supervision Law of the People’s
           法規，制定《東岳集團風險管理手冊》                           Republic of China and Interim Provisions on Banning
           《東岳集團反舞弊行為管理規定》等覆                           Commercial Bribery, the Group has formulated internal
           蓋所有分子公司的內部規章制度，堅                            business rules and regulations covering all subsidiaries,
           持對腐敗、舞弊等行為零容忍態度，                            such as Dongyue Group Risk Management Manual and
           並設立架構分明、職責清晰的監管機                            Dongyue Group Regulations on Anti-fraud Management.
           制，確保公司反腐倡廉工作有效落實，                           The Group insist on a zero-tolerance attitude towards
           打造清正廉潔的工作環境。                                corruption and fraud, and have established a supervisory
                                                       mechanism with a well-defined structure and
                                                       responsibilities to ensure the effective implementation of
                                                       the company’s anti-corruption work and to create a
                                                       corruption-free and honest working environment.

           我們持續優化集團和子公司兩級紀檢                            We continue to optimize the two-level discipline
           監察制度，逐步形成了「紀檢發現問題                           inspection and monitoring system of the Group and its
           — 管理部門研究解決方案 — 合規管                          subsidiaries, and gradually formed a working mechanism
           理制定新制度」的工作機制，由支部書                           of “discipline inspection identifies problems – the
           記擔任各單位二級紀檢監察小組組長，                           management department looks for solutions – and the
           針對各處成員及關鍵崗位進行監察，                            compliance management formulates new systems”. The
           實現高效、全面的監督管理。同時，                            branch secretary is the leader of each unit’s second-level
           集團暢通舉報信箱、舉報郵箱和舉報                            discipline inspection and monitoring team, which monitors
           電話等多樣化的溝通渠道，嚴格打擊                            the members and key positions of each division to achieve
           舉報舞弊、違紀違法及腐敗等違規行                            efficient and comprehensive supervision and
           為。2022 年，集團開通「清風東岳」公                        management. At the same time, the Group opens up a
           眾號，提供集舉報通道、普法宣傳、                            variety of communication channels, such as mailboxes,
           合規文化、警示教育於一體的服務。                            e-mail boxes and telephone numbers for whistle-blowing,
           報告期內，通過清風東岳公眾號、舉                            to strictly crack down on fraudulent reporting, disciplinary
           報郵箱等舉報渠道，我們共收到問題                            violations, corruption and other misconduct. In 2022, the
           舉 報 10 項，訪 談 一 線 幹 部 員 工 1,280              Group launched the “Qingfeng Dongyue” WeChat
           人，解決率達 100%。                                account to provide services integrating whistle-blowing

           嚴格要求相關人員參與培訓。我們組                       compliance training for Board members, management of

           結合的形式，宣貫集團制度流程和法                       for leading cadres and members of key positions and carry

           信公眾號開設「普法期刊、合規課堂」                      control, through a combination of online and offline

           裁為副主任、各部門負責人與一般員                  processes of production and operation. The Group has

           結分析並部署集團安全重點工作，保                  general managers of all subsidiaries as members, and the

           秉承統一領導、分級負責，誰發包、          regular safety potential hazards, specialized safety

5.   綠色低碳                                         5.    Green and Low Carbon
     東岳集團相信環境管理和綠色低碳是企業可                                Dongyue Group believes that environmental management and
     持續發展的根本。公司依據《中華人民共和                                green and low carbon are fundamental to sustainable corporate
     國環境保護法》等法律法規，結合企業優勢                                development. In accordance with the Environmental Protection
     資源在氣候變化應對、綠色產品開發、能源                                Law of the People’s Republic of China, and other laws and
     使用效率、資源節約舉措等方面推進可持續                                regulations, the Company has combined its advantageous
     發展，優化環保標準化管理體系，力求降低                                resources to promote sustainable development in the fields of
     業務發展過程中所產生的碳排放，踐行企業                                climate change response, green product development, energy
     公 民 的 環 保 責 任。2022 年，公 司 未 發生一                     efficiency and resource conservation measures, optimized its
     般及以上環境事件。                                          environmental protection standardized management system,
                                                        strived to reduce carbon emissions generated in the course of
                                                        business development and fulfilled its responsibility as a
                                                        corporate citizen in environmental protection. In 2022, the
                                                        Company did not have any general or above environmental
                                                        incidents.

     5.1   應對氣候變化                                       5.1   Responding to Climate Change

           東岳集團將氣候變化風險的管控作為                                   Dongyue Group regards the management and control of
           企業發展的必備能力。我們積極響應                                   climate change risks as an essential capability for corporate
           「碳達峰」「碳中和」的國家目標，通過                                 development. We are actively responding to the national
           風險管理體系，開展氣候風險識別及                                   targets of “Carbon Peak” and “Carbon Neutrality” by
           評估的工作，並針對已識別的風險採                                   identifying and assessing climate risks through our risk
           取相應的應對舉措。                                          management system and taking corresponding measures
                                                              to address the identified risks.

           •   面臨的氣候變化風險                                      •      Exposure to climate change risk

               轉型風險             政策風險           在國家雙碳目標與能源轉型趨勢的背景下，各行各業均面臨著更大的
                                               運營節能降耗的挑戰。隨著氣候變化政策的動態更新及集團業務的不
                                               斷增長，培育清潔化和低碳化的新型產業趨勢已經成為順應綠色發展
                                               趨勢、推動可持續發展的必由之路。

                                               東岳集團致力於降低因政策變化帶來的風險，積極肩負起「首批納入碳
                                               交易試點的發電企業」            的責任，加強能耗管控要求，豐富綠色產品供給。
               Transition risks Policy risks   Against the national targets of Carbon Peak and Carbon Neutrality and
                                               the trend of energy transformation, all industries are facing greater
                                               challenges to reduce energy consumption in their operations. With the
                                               dynamic update of the climate change policy and the continuous growth
                                               of the Group’s business, fostering a new trend of clean and low-carbon
                                               industries has become a necessary way to comply with the trend of green
                                               development and promote sustainable development.

                                               Dongyue Group strives to reduce the risks brought about by policy
                                               changes, actively undertakes the responsibility of being one of the “first
                                               power generation enterprises to be included in the pilot carbon trading
                                               scheme”, strengthens energy consumption control requirements and
                                               enriches the supply of green products.

                          市場風險             客戶和市場高度關注公司的應對氣候變化表現和產品碳足跡情況，尤
                                           其是針對制冷劑的生產標準要求日漸提升。東岳集團若忽視由此導致
                                           的排放量及產品碳足跡增加，則可能會導致潛在客戶的流失，影響投資
                                           者對公司市場價值的估計。
                          Market risks     Customers and the market are highly concerned about the company’s
                                           performance against climate change and the carbon footprint of its
                                           products, especially with regard to the increasing demand for higher
                                           production standards for refrigerants. If the Dongyue Group ignores the
                                           resulting increase in emissions and the carbon footprint of its products, it
                                           could lead to the loss of potential customers and affect investors’
                                           estimates of the Company’s market value.

           實體風險           物理風險             氣候變化正在影響多種氣候類型的發生強度和發生頻率，例如暴雨、颶
                                           風、沙塵暴、凝凍、極熱饑寒天氣等。這些危害將對人類的生命健康和
                                           公司日常運營造成威脅，可能導致工作場所損毀及財務損失，對公司的
                                           產能造成不利影響。
           Entity risks   Physical risks   Climate change is affecting the intensity and frequency of many types of
                                           climate, such as heavy rainfall, hurricanes, dust storms, freezing
                                           temperatures and extreme heat and cold. These hazards will pose a threat
                                           to human life and health and to the daily operations of the Company,
                                           and may result in damage to the workplace and financial loss, adversely
                                           affecting the Company’s production capacity.

       •   面臨的氣候變化機遇                                      •      Opportunities from climate change

           運營的影響，增強氣候風險管理                                        conjunction with the above risks in order to mitigate

            促進實現至 2025 年實現中水回收率不           comprehensive reclaimed water treatment and reuse,

            取適用水源方面未發生任何問題。                water resources, reducing the discharge of wastewater

     東岳集團始終秉承「以人為本」的理念，深             Dongyue Group has always adhered to the philosophy of
     知員工是推動企業可持續發展的基石。我們             “people-oriented” and understands that employees are the
     時刻關注並維護員工的合法權益，不斷完善             foundation for sustainable development of the enterprise. We
     人才培養發展體系，建立全面的福利體系，             always concern about and protect the legitimate rights and
     致力於打造平等、溫暖、共贏的工作環境。             interests of our employees, continuously improve our talent

      6.3   關愛員工生活                6.3   Caring for Employees’ Life

            東岳集團在運營中堅持以人為本，將            Dongyue Group insists on people-oriented operation and
            員工視作公司發展的重要財富。公司            regards its employees as an important asset for the
            關愛員工身心健康，提供溫馨的工作            development of the Company. The Company cares for the
            環境，努力為員工的工作生活提供全            physical and mental health of its employees, provides a
            方面的保障，豐富員工的精神文化生            warm and welcoming working environment, strives to
            活，提升員工的幸福感和歸屬感。             provide comprehensive protection for employees’ working
                                        life, enriches their spiritual and cultural life, and enhances
                                        their sense of happiness and belonging.



            薪酬福利                        Remuneration and Benefits
            東岳集團嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國勞           Dongyue Group strictly complies with the Labour Law of
            動法》等法律法規，堅持為員工提供有           the People’s Republic of China and other laws and
            競爭力的薪酬和完善的福利待遇，打            regulations, and insists on providing competitive salaries
            造涵蓋基本養老保險、基本醫療保險、           and comprehensive welfare benefits to its employees,
            失業保險、住房公積金等的基本福利            creating a basic welfare protection system covering basic
            保障體系，以及年假、節日津貼、免            pension insurance, basic medical insurance,
            費工作餐等福利，通過具有市場競爭            unemployment insurance and housing provident fund, as
            力的薪酬和多層次的福利保障，提升            well as benefits such as annual leave, holiday allowances
            員工凝聚力和歸屬感，實現員工和組            and free working meals. Through competitive salaries and
            織彼此成就、共贏與和諧發展。              multi-level welfare protection, we enhance the
                                        cohesiveness and sense of belonging of our employees
                                        and realize mutual achievement, mutual benefit and
                                        harmonious development of our employees and the
                                        organization.

            員工溝通                        Employee Communication
            東岳集團積極聽取員工的意見，搭建            Dongyue Group actively listens to the opinions of its

            發力，始終以熱忱的心態服務社會。               public welfare activities and the fight against pandemic,
                                           always serving the community with enthusiasm.

            公益活動                           Public Welfare Activities
            東岳集團秉承「建設家鄉，回報社會，              Adhering to the philosophy of “building hometown,
            報效國家，報答黨恩」的理念，圍繞環              repaying the society, serving the country, repaying the
            保、衛生、教育、體育發展及就業等               party”, Dongyue Group has carried out public welfare
            多個領域開展公益幫扶，助力構建和               support in various areas such as environmental protection,

            踐行公司的公益理念，總計用於社會               charitable funds to implement the Company’s public

ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT



    東岳教育基金 Dongyue Education Fund

    •   成立於 2000 年，旨在對本地教育教學成績突出的教師和成績優異的學生進行獎勵，並出
        資為桓台縣中小學建立「東岳書屋」。2022 年東岳教育獎勵基金總計發放獎金人民幣 179
        萬元。
        Established in 2000, the Dongyue Education Award Fund aims to reward teachers and stu-
        dents with outstanding performance in local education, and has funded the establishment
        of “Dongyue Studies” for primary and secondary schools in Huantai County. In 2022, the
        Dongyue Education Fund awarded a total of RMB1.79 million.


    東岳獎學基金 Dongyue Scholarship Fund

    •   成立於 2000 年，用於清華大學、山東理工大學對學習成績突出的優秀學生進行獎勵。
        Established in 2000, the Dongyue Scholarship Fund is designed to reward the students of
        Tsinghua University and Shandong University of Technology with outstanding academic
        achievements.

    東岳愛心基金 Dongyue Staff-caring Fund

    •   成立於 2005 年，用於困難員工幫扶。
        Established in 2005, the Dongyue Staff-caring Fund is designed to support the Group’ s
        staff in financial difficulties

    東岳尊老基金 Dongyue Elder Respecting Fund

    •   成立於 2007 年，每年為唐山鎮 80 歲以上老人發放 200 元生活補貼。
        Established in 2007, the Dongyue Elder Respecting Fund is designed to provide an allow-
        ance of RMB200 each year to elders aged over 80 in Tangshan Town.

    東岳婦幼基金 Dongyue Women and Children Fund

    •   成立於 2017 年，與桓台縣婦幼保健院聯合成立，用於周邊地區婦女兒童疾病援助和關愛。
        Established in 2017, it is jointly founded with Huantai County Women's and Children's
        Healthcare Center for the purpose of assisting and caring for women and children with
        illnesses in the surrounding areas.

附註：                                            Notes:

(a)   股份溢價                                     (a)      Share premium

      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），本公司的股份溢價賬可                         Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands, the share premium of
      分配給本公司所有者，惟在緊接建議分派股息之日後，                          the Company is distributable to the owners of the Company provided that
      本公司將有能力償還其在日常業務過程中到期的債務。                          immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to be
                                                        distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts as they fall due
                                                        in the ordinary course of business.

(b)   按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備                      (b)      Equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve

      按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備包括於報告                          The equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve comprises the cumulative net change in
      期末根據國際財務報告準則第 9 號持有之按公平值計入                        the fair value of equity instruments at FVTOCI under IFRS 9 that are held at the
      其他全面收益之股本投資之公平值累計淨變動。                             end of the reporting period.

(c)   合併儲備                                     (c)      Merger reserve

      合併儲備來自於 2006 年完成之集團重組。                            Merger reserve arose in group reorganisation completed in 2006.

(d)   股本儲備                                     (d)      Capital reserve

      於 2007 年 11 月 16 日，本公司購回所有早前已發行每股                 On 16 November 2007, the Company repurchased all of the 275,000,000
      面值 0.1 美元之普通股 275,000,000 股，而該等購回普通               previously issued ordinary shares of US$0.1 each and these repurchased ordinary
      股與截至該日期之所有法定但未發行股本一併註銷。同                          shares were cancelled with all of the authorised but unissued share capital as of
      日，藉增設 4,000,000,000 股每股面值 0.1 港元之新普通              that date. On the same date, the authorised share capital was increased to
      股，將法定股本增至 400,000,000 港元。於 2007 年 11 月            HK$400,000,000 by the creation of 4,000,000,000 new ordinary shares of
      15 日，向現有股東發行 275,000,000 股每股面值 0.1 港元             HK$0.1 each. 275,000,000 new ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each were then issued
      之新普通股。購回金額超過已發行新股份面值之款額直                          to the shareholders existing on 15 November 2007. The excess of the repurchased
      接計入股本儲備。                                          amount over the nominal amount of new shares issued was credited directly to
                                                        the capital reserve.

      向附屬公司非控股股東收購額外權益確認為與非控股權                          The acquisition of additional interest from non-controlling shareholders of
      益之交易，因此產生之相關折讓╱溢價直接於股本儲備                          subsidiaries were recognised as transactions with non-controlling interests and the
      列賬╱支銷。                                            corresponding discount/premium were credited/debited directly against capital
                                                        reserve.

(e)   安全儲備                                     (e)      Safety reserve

      根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）財政部及國家安全生產監                        Pursuant to certain regulations issued by the Ministry of Finance and the State
      督管理總局頒佈的若干法規，本集團之若干中國附屬公                          Administration of Work Safety of the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”), some of
      司須以累進比率按危險化學品銷售總收入之 0.5% 至 4%                     the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are required to set aside an amount of safety reserve
      預留一定數額之安全儲備金。該儲備可用作集團日常運                          at progressive rates from 0.5% to 4% of the total revenue from the sales of
      作於安全生產改善及維修之用，被視為開支性質，並於                          hazardous chemical. The reserve can be utilised for the spending in improvements
      產生時計入損益。                                          and maintenances of work safety on the Group’s daily operations, which are
                                                        considered expenses in nature and charged to the profit and loss as incurred.

(f)   法定盈餘儲備                                   (f)      Statutory surplus reserve

      根據中國公司法及相關公司組織章程細則，本公司中國                          In accordance with the Company Law of PRC and the relevant Articles of
      附屬公司須將相等於根據中國會計準則釐定之除稅後溢                          Association, the PRC subsidiaries of the Company are required to appropriate
      利 10% 之金額撥入法定盈餘儲備。                                amount equal to 10% of their profit after taxation as determined in accordance
                                                        with the PRC accounting standards to the statutory surplus reserve.

      法定盈餘儲備為股東權益之一部分，當其結餘達到相當                          Statutory surplus reserve is part of shareholders’ equity and when its balance
      於註冊資本 50% 之金額，則毋須進一步分配。根據中國                       reaches an amount equal to 50% of the registered capital, further appropriation is
      公司法，法定盈餘儲備可用作填補過往虧損、增加生產                          not required. According to the Company Law of the PRC, statutory surplus reserve
      及業務營運或轉增股本。                                       may be used to make up past losses, to increase production and business
                                                        operations or to increase capital by means of conversion.

3.    主要會計政策                          3.   Significant accounting policies
      報告編製依據                               Basis of preparation

      綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會頒                  The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
      佈的國際財務報告準則編製。另外，綜合財                  accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards
      務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上                  issued by International Accounting Standards Board. In addition,
      市規則及香港公司條例規定之適用披露。                   the consolidated financial statements include applicable
                                           disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of
                                           Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by
                                           the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

      綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，惟金                  The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on
      融工具於各報告期末乃按公平值計入損益及                  the historical cost basis except for financial instruments that are
      按公平值計入其他全面收益計量。歷史成本                  measured at fair value through profit or loss and fair value
      一般按交換貨品及服務之代價之公平值計算。                 through other comprehensive income at the end of each
                                           reporting period. Historical cost is generally based on the fair
                                           value of the consideration given in exchange for goods and
                                           services.

      公平值指於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交                  Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
      易中出售一項資產可收取或轉讓一項負債須                  paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
      支付之價格，不論該價格是否直接可觀察或                  market participants at the measurement date, regardless of
      可使用其他估值方法作出估計。倘市場參與                  whether that price is directly observable or estimated using
      者於計量日期對資產或負債定價時會考慮資                  another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an
      產或負債的特點，則本集團於估計資產或負                  asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the
      債的公平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財                  characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants
      務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途的公平值乃                  would take those characteristics into account when pricing the
      按此基準釐定，惟屬於國際財務報告準則第                  asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for
      2 號以股份支付款項範圍的以股份付款的交                 measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated
      易、屬於國際財務報告準則第 16 號範圍內                financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for
      的租賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若地方                  share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of
      但並非公平值的計量，譬如國際會計準則第                  IFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are
      2 號存貨內的可變現淨值或國際會計準則第                 accounted for in accordance with IFRS 16, and measurements
      36 號資產減值的使用價值除外。                     that have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value,
                                           such as net realisable value in IAS 2 Inventories or value in use in
                                           IAS 36 Impairment of Assets.

3.    主要會計政策（續）                       3.   Significant accounting policies (Continued)
      報告編製依據
           （續）                             Basis of preparation (Continued)

      此外，出於財務報告目的，公平值計量應基                  In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
      於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以及該                  measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
      等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，被歸                  degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
      入第一層、第二層或第三層的公平值級次，                  observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
      詳述如下：                                measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:

      第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產或                  Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
      負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得出；                 for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
      第二層輸入值是指由除了第一層輸入值所包                  measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
      含的報價以外的，資產或負債可由直接或間                  prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
      接觀察得出的輸入值；及第三層輸入值是指                  or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
      資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。                       unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

      主要會計政策載列如下。                          The principal accounting policies are set out below.

      綜合基準                                 Basis of consolidation

      綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其附                  The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
      屬公司控制的實體的財務報表。當本公司具                  statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
      備以下條件時，即具有控制權：                       Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                                           Company:

      —     有控制被投資方的權力；                    —     has power over the investee;

      —     可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲               —     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
            得不同回報；及                              involvement with the investee; and

      —     有能力運用其權力影響其回報。                 —     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.

      倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之一                  The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
      項或多項出現變動，本集團會重新評估其是                  facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
      否控制被投資方。                             or more of the three elements of control listed above.

      客戶合約收益                               Revenue from contracts with customers

      本集團於完成履約責任時，即於特定履約責                  The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
      任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客                  obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or
      戶時確認收益。                              services underlying the particular performance obligation is
                                           transferred to the customer.

      履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務（或                 A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
      一組貨品或服務），或一系列大致相同的可                  bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
      明確區分的貨品或服務。                          goods or services that are substantially the same.

      倘符合以下其中一項標準，則控制權隨時間                  Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
      轉移，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任                  time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction
      的進展情況而隨時間確認：                         of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following
                                           criteria is met:

      —     客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消               —     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
            耗本集團履約所帶來之利益；                        benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the
                                                 Group performs;

      —     本集團履約導致產生及提升一項資產，              —     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset
            而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控                     that the customer controls as the Group performs; or
            制；或

      —     本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代               —     the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an
            用途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取                     alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
            迄今已履約部分之款項。                          enforceable right to payment for performance completed
                                                 to date.

      否則，收益於客戶獲得特定貨品或服務的控                  Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
      制權時確認。                               customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.

      合約負債為本集團向客戶轉讓本集團已從客                  A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer
      戶收取代價（或應從客戶收取代價金額）的貨                 goods or services to a customer for which the Group has
      品或服務的責任。                             received consideration (or an amount of consideration is due)
                                           from the customer.

      租賃                                   Leases

      租賃的定義                                Definition of a lease
      倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一時期內                  A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the
      控制使用已識別資產，則該合約屬於租賃或                  right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of
      包含租賃。                                time in exchange for consideration.

      就於國際財務報告準則第 16 號初步應用日                For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of
      期或之後訂立或修訂或由業務合併產生的合                  initial application of IFRS 16 or arising from business
      約而言，本集團根據國際財務報告準則第 16                combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is or
      號中的定義，於開始、修訂日期或收購日期                  contains a lease based on the definition under IFRS 16 at
      評估合約是否為一項租賃或包含一項租賃。                  inception, modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.
      除非合約條款及條件其後出現變動，否則有                  Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and
      關合約將不予重新評估。                          conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.

      本集團作為承租人                             The Group as a lessee
      就可行實際權宜方法而言，當本集團合理預                  As a practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics are
      期對綜合財務報表的影響將不會與投資組合                  accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group reasonably
      內個別租賃有重大差異時，具有相似特徵的                  expects that the effects on the consolidated financial statements
      租賃將以組合為基礎入賬。                         would not differ materially from individual leases within the
                                           portfolio.

            金額；

            條款及條件所要求的狀況時所產生的                     the site on which it is located or restoring the underlying

                                                 conditions of the lease.

      損失計量，並對租賃負債的任何重新計量作                  depreciation and impairment losses, and adjusted for any
      出調整。                                 remeasurement of lease liabilities.

      資產擁有權的使用權資產，由開始日期起至                  obtain ownership of the underlying leased assets at the end of
      可使用年期終止時折舊。否則，使用權資產                  the lease term are depreciated from commencement date to the
      按其估計可使用年期及租期兩者中較短者以                  end of the useful life. Otherwise, right-of-use assets are
      直線法折舊。                               depreciated on a straight-line basis over the shorter of its
                                           estimated useful life and the lease term.

      本集團將使用權資產於綜合財務狀況表中呈                  The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate line item
      列為獨立項目。                              on the consolidated statement of financial position.

3.    主要會計政策（續）                       3.   Significant accounting policies (Continued)
      借貸成本                                 Borrowing costs

      借貸成本直接源自收購、建造或生產合資格                  Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,
      資產，而該等資產需要一段長時間方可供用                  construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets
      作擬定用途或出售。借貸成本會加入作該等                  that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready
      資產的成本，直至資產大致上可供用作擬定                  for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those
      用途或出售為止。                             assets until such time as the assets are substantially ready for
                                           their intended use or sale.

      於有關資產可供擬定用途或出售後仍未償還                  Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the
      的任何特定借款計入一般借貸組合以計算一                  related asset is ready for its intended use or sale is included in
      般借款的資本化率。尚未用於合資格資產的                  the general borrowing pool for calculation of capitalisation rate
      特定借貸作暫時投資賺取的投資收入，於合                  on general borrowings. Investment income earned on the
      資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。                       temporary investment of specific borrowings pending their
                                           expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing
                                           costs eligible for capitalisation.

      所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間在損益中確                  All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the
      認。                                   period in which they are incurred.

      政府補貼                                 Government grants

      在合理地保證本集團會遵守政府補貼的附帶                  Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable
      條件，以及將會得到補助前，不會確認政府                  assurance that the Group will comply with the conditions
      補貼。                                  attaching to them and that the grants will be received.

      與收入相關的政府補貼乃按系統基準，於本                  Government grants related to income are recognised in profit or
      集團將有關補助擬補償的有關成本確認為開                  loss on a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group
      支的期間內，於損益中確認。具體而言，以                  recognises as expenses the related costs for which the grants
      要求本集團購買、建造或收購非流動資產為                  are intended to compensate. Specifically, government grants of
      主要條件的政府補貼乃於綜合財務狀況表中                  which the primary condition is that the Group should purchase,
      確認為遞延收入，並在相關資產的可使用年                  construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets are recognised
      期內按系統及合理基準轉撥至損益中。                    as deferred income in the consolidated statement of financial
                                           position and transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and
                                           rational basis over the useful lives of the related assets.

      稅項                                   Taxation

      所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅項的總                  Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently
      和。                                   payable and deferred tax.

      即期應付稅項按本年度應課稅溢利計算。應                  The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.
      課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他全面收入報表中                  Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported in the
      呈報的稅前溢利不同，此乃由於其他年度的                  consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
      應課稅或可扣稅收入或開支項目，以及從未                  comprehensive income because income or expense that are
      課稅或可扣稅的項目所致。本集團的即期稅                  taxable or deductible in other years and items that are never
      項負債採用於報告期末已制定或實質制定的                  taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is
      稅率計算。                                calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
                                           substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

      遞延稅項按綜合財務報表的資產及負債賬面                  Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between
      值與用以計算應課稅溢利相應稅基的暫時差                  the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated
      額確認。遞延稅項負債通常會就所有應課稅                  financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in the
      暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資產一般在很可能                  computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are
      出現可利用暫時差額扣稅的應課稅溢利時就                  generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.
      所有可扣減暫時差額確認。若於一項交易                   Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible
      中，因商譽或因業務合併以外原因首次確認                  temporary difference to the extent that it is probable that
      其他資產及負債而引致的暫時差額既不影響                  taxable profits will be available against which those deductible
      應課稅溢利，亦不影響會計溢利，則不會確                  temporary differences can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities
      認該等資產及負債。                            are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from
                                           goodwill or from the initial recognition (other than in a business
                                           combination) of other assets and liabilities in a transaction that
                                           affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

      遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司及聯營公司                  Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary

      若本集團可控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時差額                  associates, except where the Group is able to control the
      很有可能不會於可見將來撥回的情況除外。                  reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that the

      見將來撥回時確認。                            only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will

      遞延稅項資產的賬面值於報告期末作檢討，                  The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the
      並在不再可能會有足夠應課稅溢利令全部或                  end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is
      部分資產價值被收回時作調減。                       no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be
                                           available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

      的賬面值的方式所產生的稅項結果。                     which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to

      物業、機器及設備                             Property, plant and equipment

      物業、機器及設備為持作生產或供應貨品或                  Property, plant and equipment are tangible assets that are held
      服務或作行政用途的有形資產（下文所述的                  for use in the production or supply of goods or services, or for
      在建工程除外）。物業、機器及設備乃按成                  administrative purposes (other than construction in progress as
      本減其後累計折舊及期後累計減值虧損（如                  described below). Property, plant and equipment are stated in
      有）在綜合財務狀況表內列賬。                       the consolidated statement of financial position at cost less
                                           subsequent accumulated depreciation and subsequent
                                           accumulated impairment losses, if any.

      用於生產、供應或行政用途的在建樓宇以成                  Buildings in the course of construction in progress for
      本減任何已確認減值虧損列賬。成本包括使                  production, supply or administrative purposes are carried at
      資產達致其能夠以管理層擬定的方式運作所                  cost, less any recognised impairment loss. Costs include any
      需的地點及條件而直接產生的任何成本，就                  costs directly attributable to bringing the asset to the location
      合資格資產而言，則包括根據本集團會計政                  and condition necessary for it to be capable of operating in the
      策資本化的借貸成本。該等資產按與其他物                  manner intended by management and, for qualifying assets,
      業資產的相同基準在可用作擬定用途時開始                  borrowing costs capitalised in accordance with the Group’s
      計算折舊。                                accounting policy. Depreciation of these assets, on the same
                                           basis as other property assets, commences when the assets are
                                           ready for their intended use.

      折舊利用直線法確認，以在估計可使用年期                  Depreciation is recognised so as to write off the cost of assets
      內撇銷資產（在建工程除外）的成本，再減去                 other than construction in progress less their residual values
      其剩餘價值。估計可使用年期、剩餘價值和                  over their estimated useful lives, using the straight-line method.
      折舊方法會在各報告期末進行檢討，而任何                  The estimated useful lives, residual values and depreciation
      估計變動的影響則按前瞻基準入賬。                     method are reviewed at the end of each reporting period, with
                                           the effect of any changes in estimate accounted for on a
                                           prospective basis.

      物業、機器及設備項目於出售後或當預期持                  An item of property, plant and equipment is derecognised upon
      續使用該資產將不會產生未來經濟利益時取                  disposal or when no future economic benefits are expected to
      消確認。於物業、機器及設備項目出售或報                  arise from the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss
      廢時所產生的任何盈虧（以出售所得款項與                  arising on the disposal or retirement of an item of property,
      資產賬面值的差額計算）會於損益中確認。                  plant and equipment is determined as the difference between
                                           the sales proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset and is
                                           recognised in profit or loss.

      於聯營公司的權益                             Interest in an associate

      聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力的實                   An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant
      體。重大影響力指有權參與被投資方的財務                  influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
      及經營政策的決策，但對該等政策並無控制                  financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is
      或共同控制權。                              not control or joint control over those policies.

     聯營公司之業績、資產及負債使用權益會計                   The results and assets and liabilities of associates are
     法計入此等綜合財務報表。以權益會計法處                   incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using
     理的聯營公司財務報表乃按本集團就於類似                   the equity method of accounting. The financial statements of
     情況下的交易及事件所採用者相同的會計政                   associates used for equity accounting purposes are prepared
     策編製。根據權益法，於聯營公司之投資初                   using uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for like
     步按成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後                   transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under the
     就確認本集團應佔該聯營公司之損益及其他                   equity method, an investment in an associate is initially
     全面收益而作出調整。當本集團分佔聯營公                   recognised in the consolidated statement of financial position at
     司之虧損超出本集團於該聯營公司之權益時                   cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of
     （包括實質上成為本集團於該聯營公司投資                   the profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the
     淨額一部分之任何長期權益），本集團終止                   associate. When the Group’s share of losses of an associate
     確認其所佔進一步虧損。僅於本集團已產生                   exceeds the Group’s interest in that associate (which includes
     法律或推定責任，或已代表該聯營公司支付                   any long-term interests that, in substance, form part of the
     款項之情況下，方會進一步確認虧損。                     Group’s net investment in the associate), the Group
                                           discontinues recognizing its share of further losses. Additional
                                           losses are recognised only to the extent that the Group has
                                           incurred legal or constructive obligations or made payments on
                                           behalf of the associate.

      於被投資方成為一間聯營公司當日，對聯營                  An investment in an associate is accounted for using the equity
      公司之投資採用權益法入賬。於收購一間聯                  method from the date on which the investee becomes an
      營公司之投資時，投資成本超過本集團分佔                  associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate, any
      該被投資方可識別資產及負債公平淨值之任                  excess of the cost of the investment over the Group’s share of
      何部分乃確認為商譽，並計入投資之賬面                   the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities of the
      值。本集團所佔可識別資產及負債於重新評                  investee is recognised as goodwill, which is included within the
      估後之公平淨值與投資成本之任何差額，會                  carrying amount of the investment. Any excess of the Group’s
      於收購投資期間即時於損益確認。                      share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities
                                           over the cost of the investment, after reassessment, is
                                           recognised immediately in profit or loss in the period in which
                                           the investment is acquired.

      聯營公司或合資企業進行交易所產生的溢利                  profits and losses resulting from the transactions with the
      及虧損於綜合財務報表內確認，惟僅以與本                  associate or joint venture are recognised in the consolidated

                                           associate or joint venture that are not related to the Group.

     根據國際財務報告準則第 9 號進行減值評估                    Impairment of financial assets subject to impairment assessment
     的金融資產減值                                  under IFRS 9
     本集團根據國際財務報告準則第 9 號須予減                    The Group performs impairment assessment under expected
     值的金融資產（包括貿易及其他應收款項、                      credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including trade
     已抵押銀行存款以及銀行結餘及現金）的預                      and other receivables, pledged bank deposits, and bank
     期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）進行減值評                     balances and cash) which are subject to impairment under IFRS
     估。預期信貸虧損的金額於各報告日期更                       9. The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to
     新，以反映信貸風險自初步確認以來的變動。                     reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition.

      存續期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具之預計年                     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible
      期內所有可能發生之違約事件所將產生之預                     default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.
      期 信 貸 虧 損。反 之，12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損        In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion
     （「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指預期於報告日                   of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events
      期後 12 個月內可能發生之違約事件所將產                   that are possible within 12 months after the reporting date.
      生之部分存續期預期信貸虧損。評估乃按本                     Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss
      集團過往信貸虧損經驗進行，並就與債務                      experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,
      人、整體經濟情況以及對於報告日期之當前                     general economic conditions and an assessment of both the
      情況及預測之未來情況兩者所作之評估有關                     current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast
      之特定因素作出調整。                              of future conditions.

      本集團一直就貿易應收款項確認存續期預期                     The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables.
      信貸虧損。

      就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量之虧損撥                     For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss
      備相等於 12 個月預期信貸虧損，除非自初                   allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a significant
      步確認起信貸風險顯著增加，在此情況下本                     increase in credit risk since initial recognition, in which case the
      集團將確認存續期預期信貸虧損。對於應否                     Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment of whether
      確認存續期預期信貸虧損，有關評估乃基於                     lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on significant
      自初步確認起發生違約之可能性或風險大幅                     increases in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since
      增加。                                     initial recognition.

      (i) 信貸風險大幅增加                         (i)   Significant increase in credit risk
          於評估信貸風險是否自初步確認以來                       In assessing whether the credit risk has increased
          已大幅增加時，本集團比較金融工具                       significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares
          於各報告日期出現違約的風險與該金                       the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument
          融工具於初步確認日期出現違約的風                       as at each reporting date with the risk of a default
          險。作此評估時，本集團考慮合理及                       occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of
          有理據的定量及定性資料，包括歷史                       initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group
          經驗及毋須花費過多成本或精力即可                       considers both quantitative and qualitative information
          獲得的前瞻性資料。                              that is reasonable and supportable, including historical
                                                 experience and forward-looking information that is
                                                 available without undue cost or effort.

            尤其是，評估信貸風險是否大幅增加                    In particular, the following information is taken into
            時會考慮下列資料：                           account when assessing whether credit risk has increased
                                                significantly:

            —     金融工具外部（如有）或內部信貸               —     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the
                  評級的實際或預期重大惡化；                       financial instrument’s external (if available) or
                                                      internal credit rating;

            —     信貸風險的外界市場指標的重大                —     significant deterioration in external market indicators
                  惡化，例如債務人的信貸息差大                      of credit risk, e.g. a significant increase in the credit
                  幅增加；                                spread;

            —     預期將導致債務人履行其債務責                —     existing or forecast adverse changes in business,
                  任的能力大幅下降的業務、財務                      financial or economic conditions that are expected
                  或經濟狀況的現有或預測不利變                      to cause a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability
                  動；                                  to meet its debt obligations;

            —     債務人經營業績的實際或預期重                —     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the
                  大惡化；及                               operating results of the debtor; and

            —     導致債務人履行其債務責任的能                —     an actual or expected significant adverse change in
                  力大幅下降的債務人監管、經濟                      the regulatory, economic, or technological
                  或技術環境的實際或預期重大不                      environment of the debtor that results in a
                  利變動。                                significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet
                                                      its debt obligations.

          逾期超過 30 日時，本集團假定信貸風                    Group presumes that the credit risk has increased
          險自初步確認以來已大幅增加，除非                       significantly since initial recognition when contractual

             本集團定期監控用以識別信貸風險有                     The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the

      (ii)   違約定義                          (ii)   Definition of default
             就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認                     For internal credit risk management, the Group considers
             為，違約事件在內部制訂或得自外界                     an event of default occurs when information developed
             來源的資料顯示債務人不大可能悉數                     internally or obtained from external sources indicates that
             向債權人（包括本集團）還款（未計及本                   the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the
             集團所持任何抵押品）時發生。                       Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals
                                                  held by the Group).

             不論上文為何，本集團均認為，已於                     Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default
             金融資產逾期超過 1 年後發生違約，惟                  has occurred when a financial asset is more than 1 year
             本集團有合理及具理據資料來顯示更                     past due unless the Group has reasonable and
             加滯後的違約標準更為恰當。                        supportable information to demonstrate that a more
                                                  lagging default criterion is more appropriate.

      (iii) 信貸減值金融資產                       (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets
            金融資產在一項或以上事件（對該金融                    A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more
            資產估計未來現金流量構成不利影響）                    events that have a detrimental impact on the estimated
            發生時維持信貸減值。金融資產維持                     future cash flows of that financial asset have occurred.
            信貸減值的證據包括有關下列事件的                     Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes
            可觀察數據：                               observable data about the following events:

            —     發行人或借款人的重大財務困                 —     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the
                  難；                                  borrower;

            —     違反合約，如違約或逾期事件；                —     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due
                                                      event;

            —     借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財                —     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or
                  務困難的經濟或合約理由而向借                      contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s
                  款人授予貸款人不會另行考慮的                      financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a
                  優惠；                                 concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise
                                                      consider;

            —     借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財                —     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter
                  務重組；或                               bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or

            —     該金融資產的活躍市場因財務困                —     the disappearance of an active market for that
                  難而消失。                               financial asset because of financial difficulties.

      (iv) 撇銷政策                            (iv) Write-off policy
           資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財務困難及                      The Group writes off a financial asset when there is
           無實際收回可能時（例如，當對手方處                     information indicating that the counterparty is in severe
           於清盤狀態或進入破產程序時），本集                     financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of
           團則撇銷金融資產。經考慮法律意見                      recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been
           後（倘合適），遭撇銷的金融資產可能                     placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy
           仍須按本集團收回程序進行強制執行                      proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be
           活動。撇銷構成取消確認事項。任何                      subject to enforcement activities under the Group’s
           其後收回在損益中確認。                           recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice
                                                 where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition
                                                 event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit
                                                 or loss.

      (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認                   (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL
            預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違                     The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
            約損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風                    of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
            險的函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃                     loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The
            基於根據前瞻性資料調整的歷史數據                     assessment of the probability of default and loss given
            評估。預期信貸虧損的預估乃無偏概                     default is based on historical data adjusted by
            率加權平均金額，以發生違約的風險                     forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an
            為權重確定。                               unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is
                                                 determined with the respective risks of default occurring
                                                 as the weights.

            一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根                     Generally, the ECL is the difference between all
            據合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本                     contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in
            集團預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，                    accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the
            並按初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                    Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective
                                                 interest rate determined at initial recognition.

      (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認（續）                  (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL (Continued)
          倘預期信貸虧損按集體基準計量或迎                       Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater for
          合個別工具水平證據未必存在的情況，                      cases where evidence at the individual instrument level
          則金融工具按以下基準歸類：                          may not yet be available, the financial instruments are

            —     逾期狀態；                         —     Past-due status;

            —     債務人的性質、規模及行業；及                —     Nature, size and industry of debtors; and

            —     外部信貸評級（倘有）。                   —     External credit ratings where available.

            歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以確保                    The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to
            各組別成份繼續分擔類似信貸風險特                    ensure the constituents of each group continue to share
            性。                                  similar credit risk characteristics.

            利息收入按金融資產的總賬面值計算，                   Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying
            除非該金融資產信貸減值，則利息收                    amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is
            入按金融資產的攤銷成本計算。                      credit impaired, in which case interest income is calculated
                                                based on amortized cost of the financial asset.

            本集團透過調整賬面值於損益確認所                    The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit
            有金融工具的減值收益或虧損，惟貿                    or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting their
            易及其他應收款項以及按攤銷成本計                    carrying amount, with the exception of trade and other
            量的其他金融資產除外，而相應調整                    receivables and other financial assets measured at
            乃透過虧損撥備賬予以確認。                       amortised cost, where the corresponding adjustment is
                                                recognised through a loss allowance account.

      或超過預期，或導致修訂未來應課稅溢利估                       where the actual future taxable profits generated are less or
      計的事實及情況出現變動，則可能產生重大                       more than expected, or change in facts and circumstances
      撥回或進一步確認遞延稅項資產，其將於發                       which result in revision of future taxable profits estimation, a
      生該等撥回或進一步確認期間於損益中確認。                      material reversal or further recognition of deferred tax assets
                                                may arise, which would be recognised in profit or loss for the
                                                period in which such a reversal or further recognition takes
                                                place.

      商譽減值評估                                    Impairment assessment of goodwill

      確定商譽是否減值須估計獲分配有關商譽之                       Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an
      現金產生單位（或一組現金產生單位）可收回                      estimation of the recoverable amount of the cash-generating
      金額，即使用價值或公平值減出售成本兩者                       unit (or group of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has
      間的較高者。在計算使用價值時，本集團須                       been allocated, which is the higher of the value in use or fair
      估計現金產生單位（或一組現金產生單位）預                      value less costs of disposal. The value in use calculation requires
      期產生的未來現金流量，以及選擇合適的貼                       the Group to estimate the future cash flows expected to arise
      現率以計算現值。倘實際未來現金流量低於                       from the cash-generating unit (or a group of cash-generating
      預期，或導致未來現金流量下調的事實及情                       units) and a suitable discount rate in order to calculate the
      況變動，則可能產生重大減值虧損╱進一步                       present value. Where the actual future cash flows are less than
      減值虧損。                                     expected, or change in facts and circumstances which results in
                                                downward revision of future cash flows, a material impairment

            抵押銀行存款及銀行結餘的信貸風險                     with high credit ratings assigned by international credit
            有限。管理層預計該等交易對手的不                     agencies. Management does not expect any losses from
            履約不會造成任何虧損。本集團參考                     non-performance by these counterparties. The Group
            外部信貸評級機構發佈的各個信貸評                     assessed 12m ECL for pledged bank deposits and bank
            級等級的違約概率及違約虧損相關資                     balances by reference to information relating to probability
            料評估了已抵押銀行存款及銀行結餘                     of default and loss given default of the respective credit
            的 12 個月預期信貸虧損。根據平均虧                  rating grades published by external credit rating agencies.
            損率，已抵押銀行存款及銀行結餘的                     Based on the average loss rates, the 12m ECL on pledged

            微。                                   insignificant.

            本集團業務乃按產品的類型劃分。向                     The Group’s operations are organised based on the
            本公司董事會（即總營運決策人）（「總                   different types of products. Information reported to the
            營運決策人」）報告以作資源分配及分                    board of directors of the Company, being the chief
            部表現評估的資料亦按產品及物業開                     operating decision maker (“CODM”), for the purposes of
            發的類型呈列。此乃本集團之構造基                     resource allocation and assessment of segment
            礎。                                   performance focuses on types of products and property
                                                 development. This is the basis upon which the Group is
                                                 organised.

            本集團之經營及呈報分部如下：                       The Group’s operating and reportable segments are as
                                                 follows:

            —     高分子材料；                         —    Polymers;

                  分部、高分子材料分部、有機硅                      side- products of refrigerants segment, polymers
                  分部及二氯甲烷、PVC 及燒鹼分                    segment, organic silicon segment and
                  部的副產品，物業開發及租金收                      dichloromethane, PVC and liquid alkali segment,

      於釐定非上市股本工具公平值時，本集團委                                   In determining the fair value of unlisted equity instruments, the
      聘獨立專業估值師進行有關估值。該金額乃                                   Group engages an independent professional valuer to perform
      根據估計未來現金流量貼現至其現值的現金                                   such valuation. The amount is determined based on the cash
      流量預測釐定，並需要使用主要假設，包括                                   flow projection for the estimated future cash flow discounted to
      貼現率、最終增長率、預算收益及毛利率，                                   its present value and requires the use of key assumptions,
      並考慮到相關行業增長預測及管理層就對市                                   including the discount rate, terminal growth rate, budgeted
      場發展的期望及市場方法，基於與標的公司                                   revenue and gross margin taking into account the relevant
      相似的上市公司的交易倍數，而批准的財務                                   industry growth forecasts and financial budgets approved
      預算。                                                   management’s expectation for the market development and the

      在承接任何新客戶前，本集團會調查新客戶                             Before accepting any new customer, the Group conducts
      的信譽及評估潛在客戶的信貸質素和確定客                             research on the creditworthiness of the new customer and
      戶的信用額度。客戶的信用額度和信用評級                             assesses the potential customer’s credit quality and defines
      每 年 複 核 兩 次。99%（2021 年：99%）未 逾                  credit limits by customer. Limits and scoring attributed to
      期亦未減值之貿易應收款項及應收票據擁有                             customers are reviewed twice a year. 99% (2021: 99%) of the
      信譽調查的良好信用評級。本集團視乎客戶                             trade and bills receivables that are neither past due nor impaired
      合約金額、信用及聲譽提供多種還款期。                              have the high-ranking record attributable under the research on
                                                      the creditworthiness. The Group offers various settlement terms
                                                      which vary depending on the size of contract, credibility and
                                                      reputation of the customers.

      好。                                              directors of the Company consider they are in good credit

37. 退休福利計劃                                  37. Retirement benefits plans
      本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府營                      The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of state-
      運的國家退休福利計劃的成員。本集團須向                      managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
      計劃供款指定百分比（按薪酬成本計算），作                     local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to
      為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一責任是對                      contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to the
      該等計劃作出指定供款。                              schemes to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group
                                               with respect to these schemes is to make the specified
                                               contributions.

40. 業務合併                                    40. Business combination
